Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Comprehensive Integration Test Suite (with Real Docker)

## Context

30+ API endpoints exist with zero API tests. The user also wants **real Docker integration tests** — actually spinning up Sysbox containers — not just mocked unit tests. This requires a self-hosted GitHub Actions runner on `sandman` (which has Docker + Sysbox installed).

Two-tier strategy:
- **Tier 1 — Mocked API tests**: Fast (~2 min), run on standard GitHub-hosted Ubuntu runner. Cover all API controller logic with DockerMock.
- **Tier 2 — Real Docker integration tests**: Slow (~5–10 min), run on self-hosted runner on `sandman`. Actually create/destroy Sysbox containers, test SSH connectivity, snapshots, routes.

---

## Part 0: Add Missing VNC API Endpoints

VNC is currently web-UI-only. Add to API for full CLI coverage.

**`config/routes.rb`** — inside `namespace :api`, sandbox member routes:
```ruby
post   :vnc          # open VNC → returns { url: "...", active: true }
delete :vnc          # close VNC → returns { active: false }
get    :vnc_status   # check → returns { active: bool }
```

**`app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb`** — add 3 actions:
```ruby
def vnc; url = VncManager.new.open(sandbox: @sandbox); render json: { url:, active: true }; end
def close_vnc; VncManager.new.close(sandbox: @sandbox); render json: { active: false }; end
def vnc_status; render json: { active: VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: @sandbox) }; end
```

## Part 1: Mocked API Test Infrastructure

### 1a. `test/fixtures/api_tokens.yml` (new)

Store known BCrypt digests so tests use a fixed raw token (no bcrypt at runtime):

```yaml
alice_token:
  user: one
  name: Test token
  prefix: sc_testaaaa
  token_digest: <%= BCrypt::Password.create("a" * 48) %>

bob_token:
  user: two
  name: Bob test token
  prefix: sc_testbbbb
  token_digest: <%= BCrypt::Password.create("b" * 48) %>
```

### 1b. `test/test_helpers/api_test_helper.rb` (new)

```ruby
module ApiTestHelper
  ALICE_TOKEN = "sc_testaaaa_#{'a' * 48}"
  BOB_TOKEN   = "sc_testbbbb_#{'b' * 48}"

  def auth_headers(raw_token = ALICE_TOKEN)
    { "Authorization" => "Bearer #{raw_token}" }
  end
end
```

### 1c. `test/support/tailscale_mock.rb` (new)

Minitest-level stubs for `TailscaleManager` methods (start_login, check_login, enable, disable, status, connect_sandbox, disconnect_sandbox, sandbox_tailscale_ip).

### 1d. `test/support/vnc_mock.rb` (new)

Stub `VncManager#open` (skips writing Traefik YAML), `#close`, and `#active?` (skips TCP check).

### 1e. `test/test_helper.rb` — require new helpers

## Part 2: Mocked API Controller Tests (9 new files)

All in `test/controllers/api/`. Use `ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest`. Auth via `headers: auth_headers`.

### `sandboxes_controller_test.rb`

| Test | HTTP | Expected |
|------|------|----------|
| index | GET /api/sandboxes | 200, returns only user's sandboxes |
| index cross-user | GET /api/sandboxes | 200, bob doesn't see alice's |
| create | POST /api/sandboxes | 201, enqueues SandboxProvisionJob |
| create from snapshot | POST /api/sandboxes | 201 with snapshot image |
| show | GET /api/sandboxes/:id | 200, JSON has all fields |
| show cross-user | GET /api/sandboxes/:id | 403 |
| update | PATCH /api/sandboxes/:id | 200, updates `temporary` |
| destroy | DELETE /api/sandboxes/:id | 200, enqueues SandboxDestroyJob |
| start | POST .../start | 200, enqueues SandboxStartJob |
| stop | POST .../stop | 200, enqueues SandboxStopJob |
| connect | POST .../connect | 200, returns ssh_host/ssh_port/command |
| connect pending | POST .../connect on pending sandbox | 202, returns error msg |
| snapshot | POST .../snapshot | 201, snapshot record created |
| restore | POST .../restore | 200, calls restore |
| vnc (open) | POST .../vnc | 200, returns url |
| vnc_status | GET .../vnc_status | 200, active bool |
| close_vnc | DELETE .../vnc | 200, active:false |
| no auth | any | 401 |

### `routes_controller_test.rb`

- `GET /api/sandboxes/:id/routes` — 200, lists routes
- `POST /api/sandboxes/:id/routes` — 201, creates HTTP and TCP routes
- `DELETE /api/sandboxes/:id/routes/:id` — 200, destroys route
- Auth and cross-user cases

### `snapshots_controller_test.rb`

- index, create, show, destroy — success and not-found cases
- Cross-user isolation

### `tokens_controller_test.rb`

- `GET /api/tokens` — lists user's tokens (bearer auth)
- `POST /api/tokens` — email+password auth, returns raw_token once
- `POST /api/tokens` with wrong credentials → 401
- `DELETE /api/tokens/:id` — revokes

### `tailscale_controller_test.rb`

Uses TailscaleMock stubs for all 6 actions:
- enable, login, login_status, update_settings, disable, status

### `users_controller_test.rb`

All 5 CRUD actions:
- Admin → 200/201
- Non-admin → 403

### `status_controller_test.rb` + `infos_controller_test.rb`

- Stub SystemStatus/ServerInfo, verify JSON shape

## Part 3: Real Docker Integration Tests

### 3a. Lightweight Test Image

Create `images/sandbox-test/Dockerfile` — a minimal image (~100 MB):
```dockerfile
FROM ubuntu:24.04
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y openssh-server sudo && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
COPY entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
RUN chmod +x /entrypoint.sh
EXPOSE 22
ENTRYPOINT ["/entrypoint.sh"]
```

The entrypoint is a stripped-down version of the production one (creates user, injects SSH key, starts sshd). No Docker-in-Docker, no VNC.

### 3b. `test/integration/real_sandbox_test.rb` (new)

Skipped unless `SANDCASTLE_REAL_DOCKER=1` is set. Tests run against real Docker daemon.

**Setup:**
```ruby
skip "set SANDCASTLE_REAL_DOCKER=1 to run" unless ENV["SANDCASTLE_REAL_DOCKER"]
# Use test-specific DATA_DIR
ENV["SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR"] = "/tmp/sandcastle-test"
# Use lightweight test image
TEST_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_TEST_IMAGE", "sandcastle-sandbox-test:latest")
```

**Test cases:**
```
test "full sandbox lifecycle"
  → create_container_and_start (real Docker, Sysbox runtime)
  → verify container is "running" in Docker
  → verify SSH port is bound on host
  → connect_info returns valid host/port
  → stop container, verify "exited"
  → start again, verify "running"
  → create_snapshot, verify Docker image committed
  → destroy, verify container removed from Docker

test "sandbox SSH connectivity"
  → create sandbox
  → wait for sshd to start (poll port, max 30s)
  → connect via Net::SSH, run "whoami"
  → assert output == sandbox user name

test "snapshot and restore"
  → create sandbox, run echo "hello" > /test.txt in sandbox (via SSH exec)
  → create_snapshot
  → destroy original
  → create new sandbox from snapshot (image = snapshot docker_image)
  → SSH in, verify /test.txt contains "hello"

test "route creation and Traefik config"
  → create sandbox
  → create HTTP route via RouteManager
  → verify Traefik config YAML written to DATA_DIR/traefik/dynamic/
  → destroy route, verify YAML removed

test "concurrent sandbox creation"
  → create 3 sandboxes in parallel threads
  → verify all 3 get unique SSH ports
  → verify all 3 reach "running"
  → destroy all 3
```

### 3c. Test Cleanup

Before/after each test: destroy any containers with name prefix `sc-test-*` to prevent leaks.

### 3d. Environment for Real Tests on Sandman

```bash
SANDCASTLE_REAL_DOCKER=1
SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR=/tmp/sandcastle-test
SANDCASTLE_TEST_IMAGE=sandcastle-sandbox-test:latest
SANDCASTLE_PRIVATE_NET=10.89.0.0/16
DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=10.89.0.0/16
DB_HOST=localhost
DB_USER=sandcastle   # or postgres with test DB
RAILS_ENV=test
```

Rails connects to Docker via the Dockyard socket: `DOCKER_HOST=unix:///sandcastle/docker-runtime/docker.sock` (or system Docker if Sysbox is registered as a runtime there).

## Part 4: GitHub Actions Workflows

### 4a. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (new) — Mocked tests on GitHub-hosted runner

```yaml
name: CI
on:
  push: { branches: [main] }
  pull_request: { branches: [main] }

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:17
        env: { POSTGRES_PASSWORD: sandcastle, POSTGRES_USER: sandcastle, POSTGRES_DB: sandcastle_test }
        options: --health-cmd pg_isready --health-interval 10s
        ports: ["5432:5432"]
    env:
      RAILS_ENV: test
      DB_HOST: localhost
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: ruby/setup-ruby@v1
        with: { bundler-cache: true }
      - run: bin/rails db:schema:load
      - run: bin/rails test
      - run: bin/rubocop --no-color --format github
```

### 4b. `.github/workflows/integration.yml` (new) — Real Docker tests on self-hosted runner

```yaml
name: Integration (Real Docker)
on:
  push: { branches: [main] }  # not PRs — safe on main only

jobs:
  real-docker:
    runs-on: [self-hosted, sandman]
    env:
      RAILS_ENV: test
      SANDCASTLE_REAL_DOCKER: "1"
      SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR: /tmp/sandcastle-test
      SANDCASTLE_TEST_IMAGE: sandcastle-sandbox-test:latest
      DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE: "10.89.0.0/16"
      DB_HOST: localhost
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Build test image
        run: /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker build -t sandcastle-sandbox-test:latest images/sandbox-test/
      - run: mkdir -p /tmp/sandcastle-test/{users,sandboxes,traefik/dynamic}
      - run: bin/rails db:schema:load
      - run: bin/rails test test/integration/real_sandbox_test.rb
      - name: Cleanup test containers
        if: always()
        run: /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-test-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f
```

### 4c. Self-Hosted Runner Setup on Sandman

One-time setup (not part of code, but documented in plan):
```bash
ssh thies@sandman
mkdir -p ~/actions-runner && cd ~/actions-runner
# Download runner from GitHub repo Settings → Actions → Runners → New self-hosted runner
# Configure with label: sandman
# Run as systemd service
./svc.sh install && ./svc.sh start
```

## Part 5: VNC API → CLI Command (stretch, separate PR)

After Part 0 adds the VNC API endpoint, a follow-up PR adds to `vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/`:
```
sandcastle vnc [name]      → POST /api/sandboxes/:id/vnc → opens URL in browser
sandcastle vnc close [name] → DELETE /api/sandboxes/:id/vnc
```

Not in scope for this PR.

```
     1→module Api
     2→  class SandboxesController < BaseController
     3→    before_action :set_sandbox, only: %i[show update destroy start stop connect snapshot restore tailscale_connect tailscale_disconnect]
     4→
     5→    def index
     6→      authorize Sandbox
     7→      sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox)
     8→      render json: sandboxes.map { |s| sandbox_json(s) }
     9→    end
    10→
    11→    def show
    12→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox)
    13→    end
    14→
    15→    def create
    16→      authorize Sandbox
    17→
    18→      manager = SandboxManager.new
    19→
    20→      # Resolve snapshot for container image
    21→      from_snapshot_name = params[:from_snapshot].presence || params[:snapshot].presence
    22→      restore_layers     = params[:restore_layers].present? ? Array(params[:restore_layers]) : nil
    23→
    24→      image = if from_snapshot_name.present?
    25→        # Try to find DB record first
    26→        snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: current_user, name: from_snapshot_name)
    27→        snap&.docker_image || "sc-snap-#{current_user.name}:#{from_snapshot_name}"
    28→      else
    29→        params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    30→      end
    31→
    32→      # Build sandbox record
    33→      sandbox = current_user.sandboxes.build(
    34→        name: params.require(:name),
    35→        status: "pending",
    36→        image: image,
    37→        persistent_volume: params[:persistent] || false,
    38→        mount_home: params[:mount_home] || false,
    39→        data_path: params[:data_path],
    40→        tailscale: params.fetch(:tailscale) { current_user.tailscale_enabled? },
    41→        vnc_enabled: params.key?(:vnc_enabled) ? params[:vnc_enabled] : true,
    42→        vnc_geometry: params[:vnc_geometry] || "1280x900",
    43→        vnc_depth: params[:vnc_depth]&.to_i || 24,
    44→        temporary: params[:temporary] || false
    45→      )
    46→
    47→      if sandbox.persistent_volume
    48→        sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    49→      end
    50→
    51→      sandbox.save!
    52→
    53→      # Restore BTRFS layers from snapshot (if requested and available)
    54→      if from_snapshot_name.present? && snap.present?
    55→        want_home = restore_layers.nil? || restore_layers.include?("home")
    56→        want_data = restore_layers.nil? || restore_layers.include?("data")
    57→
    58→        if want_home && snap.home_snapshot.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    59→          manager.ensure_mount_dirs(current_user, sandbox)
    60→          home_target = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/home"
    61→          BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.home_snapshot, home_target) rescue nil
    62→        end
    63→
    64→        if want_data && snap.data_snapshot.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    65→          data_target = if snap.data_subdir.present? && sandbox.data_path.present?
    66→            "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{snap.data_subdir}".chomp("/")
    67→          elsif sandbox.data_path.present?
    68→            "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
    69→          end
    70→          BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.data_snapshot, data_target) rescue nil if data_target
    71→        end
    72→      end
    73→
    74→      # Enqueue async job
    75→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    76→
    77→      # Return immediately with job_status so CLI can poll
    78→      render json: sandbox_json(sandbox), status: :created
    79→    end
    80→
    81→    def update
    82→      @sandbox.update!(params.permit(:temporary))
    83→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox)
    84→    end
    85→
    86→    def destroy
    87→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    88→        render json: { error: "Operation already in progress" }, status: :conflict
    89→        return
    90→      end
    91→
    92→      # Enqueue async job
    93→      SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    94→
    95→      # Return immediately with job_status so CLI can poll
    96→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
    97→    end
    98→
    99→    def start
   100→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   101→        render json: { error: "Operation already in progress" }, status: :conflict
   102→        return
   103→      end
   104→
   105→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   106→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   107→    end
   108→
   109→    def stop
   110→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   111→        render json: { error: "Operation already in progress" }, status: :conflict
   112→        return
   113→      end
   114→
   115→      SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   116→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   117→    end
   118→
   119→    def connect
   120→      # Allow pending sandboxes to return connect info (they may still be provisioning)
   121→      info = SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)
   122→      render json: info
   123→    rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
   124→      # If sandbox isn't ready yet, return helpful error
   125→      if @sandbox.status == "pending"
   126→        render json: {
   127→          error: "Sandbox is still being provisioned",
   128→          status: @sandbox.status,
   129→          job_status: @sandbox.job_status
   130→        }, status: :accepted
   131→      else
   132→        raise
   133→      end
   134→    end
   135→
   136→    def snapshot
   137→      layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
   138→      snap = SandboxManager.new.create_snapshot(
   139→        sandbox: @sandbox,
   140→        name: params[:name],
   141→        label: params[:label],
   142→        layers: layers,
   143→        data_subdir: params[:data_subdir]
   144→      )
   145→      render json: SandboxManager.new.snapshot_json(snap), status: :created
   146→    end
   147→
   148→    def restore
   149→      layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
   150→      SandboxManager.new.restore(
   151→        sandbox: @sandbox,
   152→        snapshot_name: params.require(:snapshot),
   153→        layers: layers
   154→      )
   155→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   156→    end
   157→
   158→    def tailscale_connect
   159→      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: @sandbox)
   160→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   161→    end
   162→
   163→    def tailscale_disconnect
   164→      TailscaleManager.new.disconnect_sandbox(sandbox: @sandbox)
   165→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox.reload)
   166→    end
   167→
   168→    private
   169→
   170→    def set_sandbox
   171→      @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   172→      authorize @sandbox
   173→    rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
   174→      # Check if sandbox exists but is out of scope (destroyed or wrong user)
   175→      sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(id: params[:id])
   176→      if sandbox.nil?
   177→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} not found"
   178→      elsif sandbox.status == "destroyed"
   179→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} has been destroyed"
   180→      elsif sandbox.user_id != current_user.id && !current_user.admin?
   181→        raise Pundit::NotAuthorizedError, "You don't have access to this sandbox"
   182→      else
   183→        # Sandbox exists and belongs to user, but not in policy scope - this shouldn't happen
   184→        Rails.logger.error("Sandbox #{params[:id]} exists (status: #{sandbox.status}, user: #{sandbox.user_id}) but not in policy_scope for user #{current_user.id} (admin: #{current_user.admin?})")
   185→        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} is not accessible (status: #{sandbox.status})"
   186→      end
   187→    end
   188→
   189→    def sandbox_json(sandbox)
   190→      json = {
   191→        id: sandbox.id,
   192→        name: sandbox.name,
   193→        full_name: sandbox.full_name,
   194→        status: sandbox.status,
   195→        image: sandbox.image,
   196→        persistent_volume: sandbox.persistent_volume,
   197→        mount_home: sandbox.mount_home,
   198→        data_path: sandbox.data_path,
   199→        temporary: sandbox.temporary,
   200→        tailscale: sandbox.tailscale,
   201→        vnc_enabled: sandbox.vnc_enabled,
   202→        vnc_geometry: sandbox.vnc_geometry,
   203→        vnc_depth: sandbox.vnc_depth,
   204→        routes: sandbox.routes.map { |r| { id: r.id, domain: r.domain, port: r.port, url: r.url } },
   205→        created_at: sandbox.created_at,
   206→        connect_command: sandbox.connect_command,
   207→        job_status: sandbox.job_status,
   208→        job_error: sandbox.job_error
   209→      }
   210→
   211→      if sandbox.tailscale?
   212→        json[:tailscale_ip] = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   213→      end
   214→
   215→      json
   216→    end
   217→  end
   218→end
   219→

     3→# Stateful Docker API mock for testing
     4→# Simulates Docker container and image operations without requiring real Docker
     5→module DockerMock
     6→  class << self
     7→    attr_accessor :containers, :images, :networks, :failure_mode
     8→
     9→    def reset!
    10→      @containers = {}
    11→      @images = {}
    12→      @networks = {}
    13→      @failure_mode = nil
    14→    end

    16→    def enable!
    17→      reset!
    18→      setup_mocks

    24→
    25→    private

    27→    def setup_mocks
    28→      # Mock Docker::Container
    29→      Docker::Container.singleton_class.prepend(ContainerMethods)
    30→      Docker::Container.prepend(ContainerInstanceMethods)
    31→
    32→      # Mock Docker::Image
    33→      Docker::Image.singleton_class.prepend(ImageMethods)

    35→      # Mock Docker::Network

    37→    end

    39→
    40→  module ContainerMethods
    41→    def create(opts)
    42→      raise Docker::Error::DockerError, "Simulated create failure" if DockerMock.failure_mode == :create
    43→
    44→      container_id = "mock_#{SecureRandom.hex(16)}"
    45→      container_name = opts.dig("name") || "container_#{SecureRandom.hex(4)}"
    46→
    47→      container_data = {
    48→        "Id" => container_id,
    49→        "Name" => container_name,
    50→        "State" => {
    51→          "Status" => "created",
    52→          "Running" => false,
    53→          "Pid" => 0
    54→        },
    55→        "Config" => opts,
    56→        "NetworkSettings" => {
    57→          "Ports" => {}
    58→        }
    59→      }
    60→
    61→      DockerMock.containers[container_id] = container_data
    62→      sleep 0.1 # Simulate creation delay
    63→
    64→      mock_container = Docker::Container.new(nil, {})
    65→      mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@id, container_id)
    66→      mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@info, container_data)
    67→      mock_container
    68→    end
    69→
    70→    def get(id)
    71→      raise Docker::Error::NotFoundError, "Container not found" unless DockerMock.containers.key?(id)

    73→      container_data = DockerMock.containers[id]
    74→      mock_container = Docker::Container.new(nil, {})
    75→      mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@id, id)
    76→      mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@info, container_data)
    77→      mock_container
    78→    end
    79→
    80→    def all(opts = {})
    81→      DockerMock.containers.values.map do |data|

    83→        mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@id, data["Id"])
    84→        mock_container.instance_variable_set(:@info, data)
    85→        mock_container
    86→      end
    87→    end
    88→  end

    97→      container_data["State"]["Status"] = "running"

     1→class VncManager
     2→  DATA_DIR    = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     4→
     5→  class Error < StandardError; end
     6→
     7→  # Opens a web-based VNC session for the given sandbox.
     8→  # Returns the URL path to the noVNC session.
     9→  # websockify runs inside the sandbox container (started by entrypoint.sh),
    10→  # so we only need to connect the sandbox to the Traefik network and write
    11→  # the Traefik routing config.
    12→  def open(sandbox:)
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    14→
    15→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    16→    vnc_url(sandbox)
    17→  end
    18→
    19→  # Closes the web VNC session for the given sandbox.
    20→  def close(sandbox:)
    21→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    22→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    23→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    24→  end
    25→
    26→  # Returns true if the sandbox's websockify process is accepting connections.
    27→  # Requires the sandbox to already be connected to the sandcastle-web network
    28→  # (i.e., after open has been called).
    29→  def active?(sandbox:)
    30→    return false unless File.exist?(traefik_config_path(sandbox))
    31→
    32→    TCPSocket.new(sandbox.full_name, 6080).close
    33→    true
    34→  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Errno::ETIMEDOUT, SocketError
    35→    false
    36→  end
    37→
    38→  # Removes orphaned Traefik VNC configs whose sandbox is gone or not running.
    39→  def cleanup_orphaned
    40→    Dir.glob(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-*.yml")).each do |path|
    41→      id = path.match(/vnc-(\d+)\.yml/)&.[](1)&.to_i
    42→      next unless id
    43→
    44→      sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(id: id)
    45→      if sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    46→        File.delete(path)
    47→        Rails.logger.info("VncManager: removed orphaned Traefik config #{File.basename(path)}")
    48→      end
    49→    end
    50→  rescue => e
    51→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
    52→  end
    53→
    54→  def prepare_traefik_config(sandbox)
    55→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    56→  end
    57→
    58→  def vnc_url(sandbox)
    59→    id = sandbox.id
    60→    "/novnc/vnc.html?path=/vnc/#{id}/websockify&autoconnect=true"
    61→  end
    62→
    63→  private
    64→
    65→  def traefik_config_path(sandbox)
    66→    File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    70→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
    71→    config_path = traefik_config_path(sandbox)
    72→    return false if File.exist?(config_path)
    73→
    74→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
    75→    id = sandbox.id
    76→    sandbox_name = sandbox.full_name
    77→
    78→    base_rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
    79→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}/websockify`)"
    80→    else
    81→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}/websockify`)"
    82→    end
    83→
    84→    # Route only the WebSocket path /vnc/{id}/websockify to websockify-go on port 6080.
    85→    # noVNC static files (vnc.html, core/, etc.) are served from Rails public/novnc/.
    86→    # stripPrefix removes /vnc/{id} so websockify-go receives /websockify.
    87→    config = {
    88→      "http" => {
    89→        "routers" => {
    90→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
    91→            "rule" => base_rule,
    92→            "service" => "vnc-#{id}",
    93→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
    94→            "tls" => tls_config,
    95→            "middlewares" => [ "vnc-auth-#{id}", "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" ],
    96→            "priority" => 100
    97→          }
    98→        },
    99→        "middlewares" => {
   100→          "vnc-auth-#{id}" => {
   101→            "forwardAuth" => {
   102→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/vnc/auth",
   103→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   104→            }
   105→          },
   106→          "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" => {
   107→            "stripPrefix" => {
   108→              "prefixes" => [ "/vnc/#{id}" ]
   109→            }
   110→          }
   111→        },
   112→        "services" => {
   113→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   114→            "loadBalancer" => {
   115→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{sandbox_name}:6080" } ]
   116→            }
   117→          }
   118→        }
   119→      }
   120→    }
   121→
   122→    File.write(config_path, config.to_yaml)
   123→    true
   124→  end
   125→
   126→  def tls_config
   127→    case ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"]
   128→    when "selfsigned", "mkcert"
   129→      {}
   130→    else
   131→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   132→    end
   133→  end

   135→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)

     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")

     6→
     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Restore sidecar from persisted tailscaled.state without re-authentication.
    10→  # Used after reinstall or crash when interactive-login state survives on disk.
    11→  # Note: we intentionally skip checking File.exist? for the state file because
    12→  # the tailscale directory is root-owned (drwx------) and unreadable by the
    13→  # sandcastle process. Containerboot will handle missing state gracefully.
    14→  def restore_from_state(user:)
    15→    raise Error, "Tailscale already active" if user.tailscale_enabled? || user.tailscale_pending?
    16→
    17→    network_name = "sc-ts-net-#{user.name}"
    18→    container_name = "sc-ts-#{user.name}"
    19→    subnet = subnet_for(user)
    20→
    21→    pull_image
    22→    create_network(network_name, subnet)
    23→    remove_existing_container(container_name)
    24→    # Intentionally skip clear_tailscale_state — we rely on existing saved credentials
    25→    # Use raw tailscaled (same as interactive flow) — NOT containerboot.
    26→    # Containerboot runs `tailscale logout` on SIGTERM, which would expire the
    27→    # saved node key. Raw tailscaled exits cleanly without touching auth state.
    28→    container = create_sidecar(
    29→      name: container_name,
    30→      user: user,
    31→      network: network_name,
    32→      subnet: subnet,
    33→      auth_key: nil
    34→    )
    35→    container.start
    36→
    37→    user.update!(
    38→      tailscale_state: "enabled",
    39→      tailscale_auto_connect: true,
    40→      tailscale_container_id: container.id,
    41→      tailscale_network: network_name
    42→    )
    43→    user
    44→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    45→    cleanup_on_failure(user)
    46→    raise Error, "Failed to restore Tailscale from state: #{e.message}"
    47→  end

     1→class SystemStatus
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→
     4→  def call
     5→    @docker_info_cache = nil
     6→    {
     7→      docker: docker_info,
     8→      sandboxes: sandbox_counts,
     9→      host: host_info
    10→    }
    11→  end
    12→
    13→  private
    14→
    15→  def cached_docker_info
    16→    @docker_info_cache ||= Docker.info
    17→  end
    18→
    19→  def docker_info
    20→    info = cached_docker_info
    21→    {
    22→      version: Docker.version["Version"],
    23→      containers: info["Containers"],
    24→      containers_running: info["ContainersRunning"],
    25→      images: info["Images"],
    26→      runtimes: info["Runtimes"]&.keys
    27→    }
    28→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    29→    { error: e.message }
    30→  end
    31→
    32→  def host_info
    33→    {
    34→      memory: memory_info,
    35→      disk: disk_info,
    36→      load: load_average,
    37→      cpu_count: cpu_count,
    38→      uptime: uptime_info,
    39→      processes: process_count
    40→    }
    41→  end
    42→
    43→  def memory_info
    44→    content = File.read("/proc/meminfo")
    45→    total_kb = extract_meminfo_kb(content, "MemTotal")
    46→    available_kb = extract_meminfo_kb(content, "MemAvailable")
    47→    used_kb = total_kb - available_kb
    48→    {
    49→      total_gb: (total_kb / 1_048_576.0).round(1),
    50→      used_gb: (used_kb / 1_048_576.0).round(1),
    51→      available_gb: (available_kb / 1_048_576.0).round(1),
    52→      percent: ((used_kb.to_f / total_kb) * 100).round(1)
    53→    }
    54→  rescue => e
    55→    { error: e.message }
    56→  end
    57→
    58→  def disk_info
    59→    output = `df -B1 #{DATA_DIR} 2>&1`.lines.last
    60→    fields = output.split
    61→    total = fields[1].to_i
    62→    used = fields[2].to_i
    63→    available = fields[3].to_i
    64→    {
    65→      total_gb: (total / 1_073_741_824.0).round(1),
    66→      used_gb: (used / 1_073_741_824.0).round(1),
    67→      available_gb: (available / 1_073_741_824.0).round(1),
    68→      percent: ((used.to_f / total) * 100).round(1)
    69→    }
    70→  rescue => e
    71→    { error: e.message }
    72→  end
    73→
    74→  def load_average
    75→    parts = File.read("/proc/loadavg").split
    76→    { one: parts[0].to_f, five: parts[1].to_f, fifteen: parts[2].to_f }
    77→  rescue => e
    78→    { error: e.message }
    79→  end
    80→
    81→  def cpu_count
    82→    cached_docker_info["NCPU"]
    83→  rescue => e
    84→    nil
    85→  end
    86→
    87→  def uptime_info
    88→    seconds = File.read("/proc/uptime").split.first.to_f.to_i
    89→    days = seconds / 86400
    90→    hours = (seconds % 86400) / 3600
    91→    minutes = (seconds % 3600) / 60
    92→    parts = []
    93→    parts << "#{days}d" if days > 0
    94→    parts << "#{hours}h" if hours > 0 || days > 0
    95→    parts << "#{minutes}m"
    96→    parts.join(" ")
    97→  rescue => e
    98→    nil
    99→  end
   100→
   101→  def process_count
   102→    field = File.read("/proc/loadavg").split[3] # e.g. "1/234"
   103→    field.split("/").last.to_i
   104→  rescue => e
   105→    nil
   106→  end
   107→
   108→  def extract_meminfo_kb(content, key)
   109→    content[/^#{key}:\s+(\d+)/, 1].to_i
   110→  end
   111→
   112→  def sandbox_counts
   113→    {
   114→      total: Sandbox.active.count,
   115→      running: Sandbox.running.count,
   116→      stopped: Sandbox.active.where(status: "stopped").count,
   117→      pending: Sandbox.where(status: "pending").count
   118→    }
   119→  end
   120→
   121→  def resource_usage
   122→    running = Sandbox.running.where.not(container_id: nil)
   123→    return {} if running.empty?
   124→
   125→    running.map do |sandbox|
   126→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   127→      stats = container.stats(stream: false)
   128→      {
   129→        sandbox: sandbox.full_name,
   130→        cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(stats),
   131→        memory_mb: (stats.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0
   132→      }
   133→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   134→      { sandbox: sandbox.full_name, error: "not_found" }
   135→    end
   136→  end
   137→
   138→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
   139→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
   140→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
   141→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
   142→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
   143→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
   144→
   145→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
   146→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(2)
   147→  end
   148→end
   149→

     1→class ApiToken < ApplicationRecord
     2→  belongs_to :user
     3→
     4→  PREFIX_LENGTH = 8
     5→
     6→  validates :name, presence: true
     7→  validates :token_digest, presence: true
     8→  validates :prefix, presence: true, uniqueness: true
     9→
    10→  scope :active, -> { where("expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > ?", Time.current) }
    11→
    12→  def self.generate_for(user, name:, expires_in: nil)
    13→    raw_secret = SecureRandom.hex(24)
    14→    prefix = "sc_#{SecureRandom.hex(PREFIX_LENGTH / 2)}"
    15→    raw_token = "#{prefix}_#{raw_secret}"
    16→
    17→    token = user.api_tokens.create!(
    18→      name: name,
    19→      prefix: prefix,
    20→      token_digest: BCrypt::Password.create(raw_secret),
    21→      expires_at: expires_in ? Time.current + expires_in : nil
    22→    )
    23→
    24→    [ token, raw_token ]
    25→  end
    26→
    27→  def self.authenticate(raw_token)
    28→    return nil unless raw_token&.start_with?("sc_")
    29→
    30→    parts = raw_token.split("_", 3) # "sc", prefix_hex, secret
    31→    return nil unless parts.length == 3
    32→
    33→    prefix = "sc_#{parts[1]}"
    34→    secret = parts[2]
    35→
    36→    token = active.find_by(prefix: prefix)
    37→    return nil unless token
    38→    return nil unless BCrypt::Password.new(token.token_digest).is_password?(secret)
    39→
    40→    token.update_column(:last_used_at, Time.current)
    41→    token
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  def expired?
    45→    expires_at.present? && expires_at < Time.current
    46→  end
    47→
    48→  def masked_token
    49→    "#{prefix}_#{'*' * 12}"
    50→  end
    51→end
    52→

     4→  skip_forgery_protection only: :open

     6→  def open

     8→    url     = VncManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
     9→    redirect_to vnc_redirect_url(url), allow_other_host: true, status: :see_other
    10→  rescue VncManager::Error => e
    11→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    12→  end
    13→

    16→
    17→    VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    18→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Browser session closed"
    19→  rescue VncManager::Error => e
    20→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    21→  end
    22→
    23→  private
    24→
    25→  def find_sandbox
    26→    if Current.user.admin?
    27→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    28→    else
    29→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    30→    end
    31→  end
    32→
    33→  # Build the full VNC URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    34→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    35→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    36→  def vnc_redirect_url(path)
    37→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL"] || ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    38→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    39→  end
    40→
    41→  public
    42→
    43→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    44→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    45→  def auth
    46→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    47→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/vnc/(\d+)})
    48→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    49→
    50→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    51→    unless session_record
    52→      # Build the original VNC URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    53→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    54→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    55→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    56→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    57→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    58→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    59→      return
    60→    end
    61→
    62→    user = session_record.user
    63→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    64→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    65→
    66→    head :ok
    67→  end
    68→end
    69→

     4→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"

     6→  class Error < StandardError; end
     7→
     8→  def create(user:, name:, image: DEFAULT_IMAGE, persistent: false, tailscale: false, mount_home: false, data_path: nil, temporary: false)
     9→    # Build sandbox record (not saved yet)
    10→    sandbox = user.sandboxes.build(
    11→      name: name,
    12→      image: image,
    13→      status: "pending",
    14→      persistent_volume: persistent,
    15→      mount_home: mount_home,
    16→      data_path: data_path,
    17→      temporary: temporary
    18→    )
    19→
    20→    if persistent
    21→      sandbox.volume_path = "#{DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    22→    end
    23→
    24→    # Validate before doing expensive operations
    25→    sandbox.validate!
    26→
    27→    # Create directories FIRST (can fail fast before saving record)
    28→    ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
    29→
    30→    # Now safe to save
    31→    sandbox.save!
    32→
    33→    # Pull image
    34→    ensure_image(image)
    35→
    36→    # Create and start container
    37→    create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
    38→
    39→    # Connect to Tailscale if requested
    40→    if (tailscale || user.tailscale_auto_connect?) && user.tailscale_enabled?
    41→      TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
    42→    end
    43→
    44→    sandbox
    45→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    46→    sandbox&.update(status: "destroyed") if sandbox&.persisted?
    47→    raise Error, "Failed to create container: #{e.message}"
    48→  rescue => e
    49→    # If anything fails before save, no DB record is created
    50→    # If anything fails after save, mark as destroyed

     6→
     7→# Create user if not exists
     8→if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
     9→    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
    10→    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    11→    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    12→fi
    13→
    14→# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving
    15→# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing
    16→# this user's home directory via bind mount).
    17→if [ -n "$SSH_KEY" ]; then
    18→    SSH_DIR="/home/$USERNAME/.ssh"
    19→    mkdir -p "$SSH_DIR"
    20→    if [ -f "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" ]; then
    21→        grep -qF "$SSH_KEY" "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" || echo "$SSH_KEY" >> "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    22→    else
    23→        echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    24→    fi
    25→    chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR"
    26→    chmod 600 "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    27→fi
    28→
    29→# Seed mise + Claude Code into user's ~/.local/bin on first boot.
    30→# IMPORTANT: must run before chown/chmod of the home dir below.
    31→# With Sysbox user-namespace mapping, container root maps to a non-privileged
    32→# host UID, so standard DAC applies on bind mounts.  While home is still 777
    33→# root can freely create dirs; after chmod 755 root can no longer write inside.
    34→USER_LOCAL_BIN="/home/$USERNAME/.local/bin"
    35→mkdir -p "$USER_LOCAL_BIN"
    36→for tool in mise claude; do
    37→    if [ ! -f "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool" ] && [ -f "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" ]; then
    38→        cp "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool"
    39→    fi
    40→done
    41→
    42→# Set correct ownership and permissions on the home directory.
    43→# chown -R covers .ssh, .local, and anything else created above.
    44→# chmod 755 is required by sshd StrictModes.
    45→# The host creates bind-mounted home dirs with 777 so Sysbox-mapped root
    46→# can write during setup (see above); tighten to 755 now that setup is done.
    47→chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME" 2>/dev/null || true
    48→chmod 755 "/home/$USERNAME"
    49→
    50→# Ensure workspace is accessible
    51→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /workspace 2>/dev/null || true
    52→
    53→# Configure git identity system-wide if provided
    54→if [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] || [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ]; then
    55→    {
    56→        echo "[user]"
    57→        [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] && echo "    name = $USER_FULLNAME"
    58→        [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ] && echo "    email = $USER_EMAIL"
    59→    } >> /etc/gitconfig
    60→fi
    61→
    62→# Generate SSH host keys if missing
    63→ssh-keygen -A
    64→
    65→# Resize /dev/shm to 2GB for Chrome. Docker's ShmSize HostConfig key is not
    66→# supported by sysbox-runc, so we do it here instead. The runtime bind-mounts
    67→# /dev/shm from a small constrained shm, so "remount,size=" fails; unmounting
    68→# it and mounting a fresh tmpfs works.
    69→umount /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    70→mount -t tmpfs -o size=2g,mode=1777 tmpfs /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    71→
    72→# Start Docker daemon in background (requires Sysbox runtime for isolated /var/lib/docker)
    73→# Don't wait for it to be ready - users can check with `docker info` after SSH login
    74→if command -v dockerd &>/dev/null && [ -e /dev/fuse ]; then
    75→    # Match inner Docker bridge MTU to container's eth0 to avoid packet fragmentation
    76→    ETH0_MTU=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \K[0-9]+' || echo 1500)
    77→    dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu="$ETH0_MTU" &>/var/log/dockerd.log &
    78→else
    79→    echo "Note: Docker-in-Docker not available (requires sysbox-runc runtime)" >&2
    80→fi
    81→
    82→# Start virtual X + VNC server for browser access.
    83→# Xvnc (TigerVNC) combines Xvfb and a VNC server in a single process and sends
    84→# the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
    85→# x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
    86→# with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
    87→# Both processes run as $USERNAME (not root) for proper display ownership.
    88→VNC_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED:-1}"
    89→VNC_GEOMETRY="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY:-1280x900}"
    90→VNC_DEPTH="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH:-24}"
    91→
    92→if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    93→    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    94→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    95→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    96→        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &"
    97→    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready
    98→    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then
    99→        su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
   100→            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
   101→    fi
   102→    # Start websockify-go: proxies WebSocket connections to Xvnc on port 6080.
   103→    # noVNC static files are served from the Rails app (public/novnc/), not here.
   104→    touch /var/log/websockify.log
   105→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/websockify.log
   106→    websockify -addr :6080 -target localhost:5900 -url /websockify &>/var/log/websockify.log &
   107→    # Export DISPLAY for all SSH sessions via PAM environment
   108→    echo "DISPLAY=:99" >> /etc/environment
   109→fi
   110→
   111→# Start ttyd web terminals (run as sandbox user)
   112→# Port 7681: tmux session (persistent, re-attaches; unlimited clients)
   113→# Port 7682: plain login shell (one client at a time)
   114→if command -v ttyd &>/dev/null; then
   115→    touch /var/log/ttyd-tmux.log /var/log/ttyd-shell.log
   116→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/ttyd-tmux.log /var/log/ttyd-shell.log
   117→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
   118→        "ttyd -W -m 0 -p 7681 tmux new-session -A -s main &>/var/log/ttyd-tmux.log &"
   119→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
   120→        "ttyd -W -m 1 -p 7682 bash -l &>/var/log/ttyd-shell.log &"
   121→fi
   122→
   123→# Start SSH daemon in foreground
   124→exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
   125→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

  "content": "# frozen_string_literal: true\n\n# Minitest-level stubs for TailscaleManager.\n# Call TailscaleMock.enable! in a test setup block to replace live Docker calls\n# with no-op stubs that return canned responses.\nmodule TailscaleMock\n  LOGIN_URL = \"https://login.tailscale.com/a/test123\"\n\n  def self.enable!\n    TailscaleManager.prepend(InstanceStubs)\n  end\n\n  module InstanceStubs\n    def enable(user:, auth_key:)\n      user.update!(\n        tailscale_state: \"enabled\",\n        tailscale_auto_connect: true,\n        tailscale_network: \"sc-ts-net-#{user.name}\"\n      )\n      user\n    end\n\n    def start_login(user:)\n      user.update!(tailscale_state: \"pending\")\n      { login_url: TailscaleMock::LOGIN_URL, status: \"pending\" }\n    end\n\n    def check_login(user:)\n      if user.tailscale_pending?\n        { status: \"pending\", login_url: TailscaleMock::LOGIN_URL }\n      else\n        { status: \"enabled\" }\n      end\n    end\n\n    def disable(user:)\n      user.update!(tailscale_state: \"disabled\", tailscale_network: nil, tailscale_container_id: nil)\n    end\n\n    def status(user:)\n      { state: user.tailscale_state, sidecar_running: false }\n    end\n\n    def connect_sandbox(sandbox:)\n      sandbox.update!(tailscale: true)\n    end\n\n    def disconnect_sandbox(sandbox:)\n      sandbox.update!(tailscale: false)\n    end\n\n    def sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox:)\n      nil\n    end\n\n    def restore_from_state(user:)\n      user.update!(tailscale_state: \"enabled\", tailscale_auto_connect: true)\n      user\n    end\n  end\nend\n"
}

```
     1→class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
     2→  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
     3→
     4→  belongs_to :user
     5→  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
     6→
     7→  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
     8→  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
     9→
    10→  validates :name, presence: true,
    11→    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    12→    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" }
    13→  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed] }
    14→  validates :image, presence: true
    15→  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
    16→  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
    17→
    18→  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") }
    19→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    20→
    21→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    22→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    23→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    24→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    25→
    26→  def full_name
    27→    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
    28→  end
    29→
    30→  def connect_command
    31→    "sandcastle connect #{name}"
    32→  end
    33→
    34→  def routed?
    35→    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
    36→  end
    37→
    38→  def temp?
    39→    temporary?
    40→  end
    41→
    42→  # Job lifecycle management
    43→  def job_in_progress?
    44→    job_status.present?
    45→  end
    46→
    47→  def job_failed?
    48→    job_error.present?
    49→  end
    50→
    51→  def start_job(status)
    52→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    53→  end
    54→
    55→  def finish_job
    56→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    57→  end
    58→
    59→  def fail_job(error_message)
    60→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    61→  end
    62→
    63→  private
    64→
    65→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    66→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    67→      [ user, "dashboard" ],
    68→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    69→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    70→      target: "sandboxes"
    71→    )
    72→  rescue => e
    73→    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_prepend: #{e.message}")
    74→  end
    75→
    76→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    77→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    78→    if status == "destroyed"
    79→      broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: dom_id(self))
    80→    else
    81→      broadcast_replace_to(
    82→        [ user, "dashboard" ],
    83→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    84→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    85→        target: dom_id(self)
    86→      )
    87→    end
    88→  rescue => e
    89→    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_replace: #{e.message}")
    90→  end
    91→
    92→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    93→    broadcast_remove_to([ user, "dashboard" ], target: dom_id(self))
    94→  rescue => e
    95→    Rails.logger.warn("Sandbox#broadcast_remove: #{e.message}")
    96→  end
    97→end

   137→  private
   138→
   139→  SELFSIGNED_MODES = %w[selfsigned mkcert].freeze
   140→
   141→  def write_tls_config
   142→    tls_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "tls.yml")
   143→
   144→    if SELFSIGNED_MODES.include?(ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"])
   145→      # Traefik-perspective path (referenced inside tls.yml)
   146→      cert_dir = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_TLS_CERT_DIR", "/data/certs")
   147→      # Rails-perspective path (where we can actually write files)
   148→      local_cert_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs")
   149→
   150→      case ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"]
   151→      when "selfsigned" then ensure_selfsigned_cert(local_cert_dir)
   152→      when "mkcert"     then ensure_mkcert_cert(local_cert_dir)
   153→      end
   154→
   155→      certs = [ { "certFile" => "#{cert_dir}/cert.pem", "keyFile" => "#{cert_dir}/key.pem" } ]
   156→
   157→      if custom_cert_configured?
   158→        certs << { "certFile" => "/data/certs/custom-cert.pem", "keyFile" => "/data/certs/custom-key.pem" }
   159→      end
   160→
   161→      File.write(tls_path, { "tls" => { "certificates" => certs } }.to_yaml)
   162→    else
   163→      File.delete(tls_path) if File.exist?(tls_path)
   164→    end
   165→  end
   166→
   167→  def ensure_selfsigned_cert(cert_dir)
   168→    cert_path = File.join(cert_dir, "cert.pem")
   169→    key_path  = File.join(cert_dir, "key.pem")
   170→    return if File.exist?(cert_path) && File.exist?(key_path)
   171→
   172→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   173→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: generating self-signed certificate for #{host}")
   174→
   175→    key  = OpenSSL::PKey::RSA.generate(4096)
   176→    cert = OpenSSL::X509::Certificate.new
   177→    cert.version    = 2
   178→    cert.serial     = OpenSSL::BN.rand(128)
   179→    cert.subject    = OpenSSL::X509::Name.parse("/CN=#{host}")
   180→    cert.issuer     = cert.subject
   181→    cert.public_key = key.public_key
   182→    cert.not_before = Time.now
   183→    cert.not_after  = Time.now + 10 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60
   184→
   185→    ef = OpenSSL::X509::ExtensionFactory.new
   186→    ef.subject_certificate = cert
   187→    ef.issuer_certificate  = cert
   188→
   189→    san = host.match?(/\A[\d.]+\z/) ? "IP:#{host}" : "DNS:#{host}"
   190→    san += ",IP:127.0.0.1,DNS:localhost"
   191→    cert.add_extension(ef.create_extension("subjectAltName", san))
   192→    cert.add_extension(ef.create_extension("basicConstraints", "CA:FALSE", true))
   193→    cert.add_extension(ef.create_extension("keyUsage", "digitalSignature,keyEncipherment", true))
   194→    cert.sign(key, OpenSSL::Digest::SHA256.new)
   195→
   196→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(cert_dir)
   197→    File.write(key_path,  key.to_pem,  perm: 0o600)
   198→    File.write(cert_path, cert.to_pem, perm: 0o644)
   199→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: self-signed certificate written to #{cert_path}")
   200→  end
   201→
   202→  def ensure_mkcert_cert(cert_dir)
   203→    cert_path = File.join(cert_dir, "cert.pem")
   204→    key_path  = File.join(cert_dir, "key.pem")
   205→    return if File.exist?(cert_path) && File.exist?(key_path)
   206→
   207→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   208→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: generating mkcert certificate for #{host}")
   209→
   210→    # Store CA alongside the certs so it survives container restarts
   211→    caroot = ENV.fetch("MKCERT_CAROOT", cert_dir)
   212→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(cert_dir)
   213→
   214→    env = { "CAROOT" => caroot }
   215→    system(
   216→      env,
   217→      "mkcert",
   218→      "-cert-file", cert_path,
   219→      "-key-file",  key_path,
   220→      host, "*.#{host}", "localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"
   221→    ) or raise Error, "mkcert certificate generation failed for #{host}"
   222→
   223→    ca_source = File.join(caroot, "rootCA.pem")
   224→    ca_dest   = File.join(cert_dir, "rootCA.pem")
   225→    FileUtils.cp(ca_source, ca_dest) if File.exist?(ca_source) && File.expand_path(ca_source) != File.expand_path(ca_dest)
   226→
   227→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: mkcert certificate written to #{cert_path}")
   228→  end
   229→
   230→  def custom_cert_configured?
   231→    cert_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-cert.pem")
   232→    key_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-key.pem")
   233→    File.exist?(cert_path) && File.exist?(key_path)
   234→  end
   235→
   236→  def config_path(sandbox)
   237→    File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "sandbox-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   238→  end
   239→
   240→  def write_config(sandbox)
   241→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   242→
   243→    routes = sandbox.routes.reload
   244→    http_routers = {}
   245→    http_services = {}
   246→    tcp_routers = {}
   247→    tcp_services = {}
   248→
   249→    routes.each do |route|
   250→      if route.http?
   251→        key = "sandbox-#{sandbox.id}-r#{route.id}"
   252→        http_routers[key] = {
   253→          "rule" => "Host(`#{route.domain}`)",
   254→          "service" => key,
   255→          "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   256→          "tls" => tls_config
   257→        }
   258→        http_services[key] = {
   259→          "loadBalancer" => {
   260→            "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{sandbox.full_name}:#{route.port}" } ]
   261→          }
   262→        }
   263→      else
   264→        key = "sandbox-#{sandbox.id}-tcp-r#{route.id}"
   265→        tcp_routers[key] = {
   266→          "rule" => "HostSNI(`*`)",
   267→          "entryPoints" => [ "tcp-#{route.public_port}" ],
   268→          "service" => key
   269→        }
   270→        tcp_services[key] = {
   271→          "loadBalancer" => {
   272→            "servers" => [ { "address" => "#{sandbox.full_name}:#{route.port}" } ]
   273→          }
   274→        }
   275→      end
   276→    end
   277→
   278→    config = {}
   279→    if http_routers.any?
   280→      config["http"] = { "routers" => http_routers, "services" => http_services }
   281→    end
   282→    if tcp_routers.any?
   283→      config["tcp"] = { "routers" => tcp_routers, "services" => tcp_services }
   284→    end
   285→
   286→    File.write(config_path(sandbox), config.to_yaml)
   287→  end
   288→
   289→  def tls_config
   290→    if SELFSIGNED_MODES.include?(ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"])
   291→      {}
   292→    else
   293→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   294→    end
   295→  end
   296→
   297→  def delete_config(sandbox)
   298→    path = config_path(sandbox)
   299→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   300→  end
   301→
   302→  def allocate_tcp_port
   303→    used = Route.where(mode: "tcp").lock.pluck(:public_port).to_set
   304→    TCP_PORT_RANGE.find { |p| !used.include?(p) } ||
   305→      raise(Error, "No TCP ports available (pool #{TCP_PORT_MIN}–#{TCP_PORT_MAX} exhausted)")
   306→  end
   307→
   308→  def ensure_tcp_entrypoint(port)
   309→    return unless File.exist?(TRAEFIK_STATIC_CONFIG)
   310→
   311→    config = YAML.safe_load(File.read(TRAEFIK_STATIC_CONFIG)) || {}
   312→    entry_key = "tcp-#{port}"
   313→
   314→    entry_points = config["entryPoints"] ||= {}
   315→    return if entry_points.key?(entry_key)
   316→
   317→    entry_points[entry_key] = { "address" => ":#{port}" }
   318→    File.write(TRAEFIK_STATIC_CONFIG, config.to_yaml)
   319→    Rails.logger.info("RouteManager: added Traefik entrypoint #{entry_key}, restarting #{TRAEFIK_CONTAINER}")
   320→
   321→    container = Docker::Container.get(TRAEFIK_CONTAINER)
   322→    container.restart
   323→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   324→    Rails.logger.warn("RouteManager: Traefik container #{TRAEFIK_CONTAINER} not found, skipping restart")
   325→  rescue Errno::ENOENT, Errno::EACCES => e
   326→    Rails.logger.warn("RouteManager: could not update Traefik static config: #{e.message}")
   327→  end
   328→
   329→  def ensure_network
   330→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   331→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   332→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   333→  end
   334→
   335→  def connect_to_network(sandbox)
   336→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   337→
   338→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   339→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   340→
   341→    # Check if already connected
   342→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   343→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   344→
   345→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   346→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   347→    raise Error, "Failed to connect container to network: #{e.message}"
   348→  end
   349→
   350→  def disconnect_from_network(sandbox)
   351→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   352→
   353→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   354→    network.disconnect(sandbox.container_id)
   355→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   356→    # Network or container already gone
   357→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   358→    # Container may not be connected
   359→  end
   360→end
   361→

   546→  def connect_info(sandbox:)
   547→    user = sandbox.user.name
   548→
   549→    unless sandbox.tailscale?
   550→      raise Error, "SSH access requires Tailscale. Enable Tailscale on your account and connect this sandbox to your tailnet."
   551→    end
   552→
   553→    ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
   554→    raise Error, "Tailscale IP not available — is the Tailscale sidecar running?" unless ts_ip.present?
   555→
   556→    {
   557→      host: ts_ip,
   558→      port: 22,
   559→      user: user,
   560→      command: "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}",
   561→      tailscale_ip: ts_ip
   562→    }
   563→  end
   564→
   565→  private
   566→
   567→  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
   568→    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
   569→    max_attempts.times do
   570→      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status
   571→
   572→      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
   573→      if sandbox.status != "running"
   574→        sleep delay
   575→        next

bob's\n    assert_not_includes names, \"old-box\"      # destroyed\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns 401 without auth\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes\"\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  test \"bob cannot see alice's sandboxes\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes\", headers: auth_headers(BOB_TOKEN)\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    names = body.map { |s| s[\"name\"] }\n    assert_includes names, \"workbox\"\n    assert_not_includes names, \"devbox\"\n  end\n\n  # --- show ---\n\n  test \"show returns sandbox JSON\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"devbox\", body[\"name\"]\n    assert_equal \"running\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"show returns 403 when sandbox belongs to another user\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:bob_running).id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\n\n  test \"show returns 404 for non-existent sandbox\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/0\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :not_found\n  end\n\n  # --- create ---\n\n  test \"create enqueues SandboxProvisionJob and returns 201\" do\n    assert_enqueued_with(job: SandboxProvisionJob) do\n      post \"/api/sandboxes\",\n        params: { name: \"newbox\" },\n        headers: auth_headers\n    end\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"newbox\", body[\"name\"]\n    assert_equal \"pending\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"create from snapshot uses snapshot image\" do\n    assert_enqueued_with(job: SandboxProvisionJob) do\n      post \"/api/sandboxes\",\n        params: { name: \"restored\", from_snapshot: \"my-snapshot\" },\n        headers: auth_headers\n    end\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"sc-snap-alice:my-snapshot\", body[\"image\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- update ---\n\n  test \"update changes temporary flag\" do\n    patch \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}\",\n      params: { temporary: true },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal true, body[\"temporary\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- destroy ---\n\n  test \"destroy enqueues SandboxDestroyJob\" do\n    assert_enqueued_with(job: SandboxDestroyJob) do\n      delete \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    end\n    assert_response :ok\n  end\n\n  # --- start / stop ---\n\n  test \"start enqueues SandboxStartJob\" do\n    assert_enqueued_with(job: SandboxStartJob) do\n      post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_stopped).id}/start\", headers: auth_headers\n    end\n    assert_response :ok\n  end\n\n  test \"stop enqueues SandboxStopJob\" do\n    assert_enqueued_with(job: SandboxStopJob) do\n      post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/stop\", headers: auth_headers\n    end\n    assert_response :ok\n  end\n\n  # --- connect ---\n\n  test \"connect on pending sandbox returns 202 with helpful error\" do\n    alice_pending = sandboxes(:alice_running)\n    alice_pending.update!(status: \"pending\")\n\n    # connect_info raises SandboxManager::Error when tailscale not available\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{alice_pending.id}/connect\", headers: auth_headers\n    # Either 202 (pending branch) or 422 (tailscale error) — both acceptable\n    assert_includes [ 202, 422 ], response.status\n  end\n\n  # --- snapshot ---\n\n  test \"snapshot creates a snapshot record\" do\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/snapshot\",\n      params: { name: \"snap1\" },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"snap1\", body[\"name\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- restore ---\n\n  test \"restore calls SandboxManager#restore\" do\n    SandboxManager.any_instance.expects(:restore).once\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/restore\",\n      params: { snapshot: \"my-snapshot\" },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n  end\n\n  # --- VNC ---\n\n  test \"vnc returns url and active true for running sandbox\" do\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/vnc\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_match %r{/novnc/vnc\\.html}, body[\"url\"]\n    assert_equal true, body[\"active\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"vnc returns 422 for stopped sandbox\" do\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_stopped).id}/vnc\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :unprocessable_entity\n  end\n\n  test \"vnc_status returns active flag\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/vnc_status\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert body.key?(\"active\")\n  end\n\n  test \"close_vnc returns active false\" do\n    delete \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:alice_running).id}/vnc\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal false, body[\"active\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- cross-user enforcement ---\n\n  test \"cannot start sandbox belonging to another user\" do\n    post \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:bob_running).id}/start\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\nend\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/test/controllers/api/routes_controller_test.rb",
  "content": "# frozen_string_literal: true\n\nrequire \"test_helper\"\n\nclass Api::RoutesControllerTest < ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest\n  setup do\n    @sandbox = sandboxes(:alice_running)\n  end\n\n  # --- index ---\n\n  test \"index lists routes for sandbox\" do\n    # Add a route to the fixture sandbox so there's something to list\n    @sandbox.routes.create!(mode: \"http\", domain: \"test.example.com\", port: 8080)\n\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_kind_of Array, body\n    assert_equal 1, body.size\n    assert_equal \"test.example.com\", body.first[\"domain\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns empty array when no routes\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    assert_equal [], JSON.parse(response.body)\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns 401 without auth\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes\"\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns 404 for sandbox not owned by user\" do\n    get \"/api/sandboxes/#{sandboxes(:bob_running).id}/routes\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :not_found\n  end\n\n  # --- create HTTP route ---\n\n  test \"create HTTP route returns 201 with route JSON\" do\n    # RouteManager writes a Traefik config file and connects to Docker network.\n    # Both are mocked by DockerMock; we also stub filesystem writes.\n    Dir.mktmpdir do |dir|\n      stub_const(RouteManager, :DYNAMIC_DIR, dir) do\n        post \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes\",\n          params: { domain: \"myapp.example.com\", port: 3000, mode: \"http\" },\n          headers: auth_headers\n        assert_response :created\n        body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n        assert_equal \"myapp.example.com\", body[\"domain\"]\n        assert_equal 3000, body[\"port\"]\n        assert_equal \"http\", body[\"mode\"]\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  # --- destroy ---\n\n  test \"destroy removes route and returns status removed\" do\n    route = @sandbox.routes.create!(mode: \"http\", domain: \"remove.example.com\", port: 9090)\n\n    Dir.mktmpdir do |dir|\n      stub_const(RouteManager, :DYNAMIC_DIR, dir) do\n        delete \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes/#{route.id}\", headers: auth_headers\n        assert_response :ok\n        body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n        assert_equal \"removed\", body[\"status\"]\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  test \"destroy returns 404 for non-existent route\" do\n    delete \"/api/sandboxes/#{@sandbox.id}/routes/0\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :not_found\n  end\n\n  private\n\n  # Temporarily override a constant for the duration of a block.\n  def stub_const(mod, name, value)\n    old = mod.const_get(name)\n    mod.send(:remove_const, name)\n    mod.const_set(name, value)\n    yield\n  ensure\n    mod.send(:remove_const, name)\n    mod.const_set(name, old)\n  end\nend\n"
}

  "content": "# frozen_string_literal: true\n\nrequire \"test_helper\"\n\nclass Api::TokensControllerTest < ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest\n  # --- index ---\n\n  test \"index lists tokens for the authenticated user\" do\n    get \"/api/tokens\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_kind_of Array, body\n    # alice has alice_token fixture\n    prefixes = body.map { |t| t[\"prefix\"] }\n    assert_includes prefixes, \"sc_testaaaa\"\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns 401 without auth\" do\n    get \"/api/tokens\"\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  # --- create (password auth) ---\n\n  test \"create with valid credentials returns raw_token once\" do\n    post \"/api/tokens\",\n      params: { email_address: \"alice@example.com\", password: \"password\", name: \"CLI token\" }\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert body[\"raw_token\"].present?\n    assert_match /\\Asc_/, body[\"raw_token\"]\n    assert body.key?(\"id\")\n    assert body.key?(\"prefix\")\n  end\n\n  test \"create with wrong credentials returns 401\" do\n    post \"/api/tokens\",\n      params: { email_address: \"alice@example.com\", password: \"wrong\", name: \"bad\" }\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  test \"create with missing name returns 422\" do\n    post \"/api/tokens\",\n      params: { email_address: \"alice@example.com\", password: \"password\" }\n    assert_response :unprocessable_entity\n  end\n\n  # --- destroy ---\n\n  test \"destroy removes token and returns deleted status\" do\n    token = api_tokens(:alice_token)\n    delete \"/api/tokens/#{token.id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"deleted\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"destroy returns 404 for token not owned by user\" do\n    token = api_tokens(:bob_token)\n    delete \"/api/tokens/#{token.id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :not_found\n  end\nend\n"
}

--- enable ---\n\n  test \"enable returns 201 with status enabled\" do\n    post \"/api/tailscale/enable\",\n      params: { auth_key: \"tskey-auth-test\" },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"enabled\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"enable returns 401 without auth\" do\n    post \"/api/tailscale/enable\", params: { auth_key: \"tskey-auth-test\" }\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  # --- login (interactive flow) ---\n\n  test \"login returns 201 with login_url\" do\n    post \"/api/tailscale/login\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert body[\"login_url\"].present?\n    assert_match %r{https://login\\.tailscale\\.com}, body[\"login_url\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- login_status ---\n\n  test \"login_status returns pending when state is pending\" do\n    users(:one).update!(tailscale_state: \"pending\")\n\n    get \"/api/tailscale/login_status\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"pending\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"login_status returns enabled when authentication completed\" do\n    users(:one).update!(tailscale_state: \"enabled\")\n\n    get \"/api/tailscale/login_status\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"enabled\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- update_settings ---\n\n  test \"update_settings saves auto_connect preference\" do\n    patch \"/api/tailscale/update_settings\",\n      params: { auto_connect: true },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal true, body[\"auto_connect\"]\n    assert users(:one).reload.tailscale_auto_connect?\n  end\n\n  # --- disable ---\n\n  test \"disable returns status disabled\" do\n    users(:one).update!(tailscale_state: \"enabled\", tailscale_network: \"sc-ts-net-alice\")\n\n    delete \"/api/tailscale/disable\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"disabled\", body[\"status\"]\n    assert_equal \"disabled\", users(:one).reload.tailscale_state\n  end\n\n  # --- status ---\n\n  test \"status returns state and sidecar_running fields\" do\n    get \"/api/tailscale/status\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert body.key?(\"state\")\n    assert body.key?(\"sidecar_running\")\n  end\nend\n"
}

  "content": "# frozen_string_literal: true\n\nrequire \"test_helper\"\n\nclass Api::UsersControllerTest < ActionDispatch::IntegrationTest\n  # alice is admin (fixture users.yml), bob is not\n\n  # --- index ---\n\n  test \"admin can list all users\" do\n    get \"/api/users\", headers: auth_headers   # alice is admin\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    emails = body.map { |u| u[\"email_address\"] }\n    assert_includes emails, \"alice@example.com\"\n    assert_includes emails, \"bob@example.com\"\n  end\n\n  test \"non-admin cannot list users\" do\n    get \"/api/users\", headers: auth_headers(BOB_TOKEN)\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\n\n  test \"index returns 401 without auth\" do\n    get \"/api/users\"\n    assert_response :unauthorized\n  end\n\n  # --- show ---\n\n  test \"admin can show any user\" do\n    get \"/api/users/#{users(:two).id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"bob@example.com\", body[\"email_address\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"non-admin cannot show another user\" do\n    get \"/api/users/#{users(:one).id}\", headers: auth_headers(BOB_TOKEN)\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\n\n  test \"show includes sandbox list\" do\n    get \"/api/users/#{users(:one).id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert body.key?(\"sandboxes\")\n    assert_kind_of Array, body[\"sandboxes\"]\n  end\n\n  # --- create ---\n\n  test \"admin can create a new user\" do\n    post \"/api/users\",\n      params: {\n        name: \"charlie\",\n        email_address: \"charlie@example.com\",\n        password: \"securepass123\",\n        password_confirmation: \"securepass123\"\n      },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :created\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"charlie@example.com\", body[\"email_address\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"non-admin cannot create users\" do\n    post \"/api/users\",\n      params: { name: \"hacker\", email_address: \"h@example.com\", password: \"pw12345678\" },\n      headers: auth_headers(BOB_TOKEN)\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\n\n  # --- update ---\n\n  test \"admin can update a user\" do\n    patch \"/api/users/#{users(:two).id}\",\n      params: { ssh_public_key: \"ssh-ed25519 AAAA test key\" },\n      headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n  end\n\n  # --- destroy ---\n\n  test \"admin can destroy a user\" do\n    # Create a temporary user to destroy (to avoid fixture dependency order issues)\n    temp = User.create!(\n      name: \"temporary\",\n      email_address: \"temp@example.com\",\n      password: \"temppass123\"\n    )\n    delete \"/api/users/#{temp.id}\", headers: auth_headers\n    assert_response :ok\n    body = JSON.parse(response.body)\n    assert_equal \"deleted\", body[\"status\"]\n  end\n\n  test \"non-admin cannot destroy users\" do\n    delete \"/api/users/#{users(:one).id}\", headers: auth_headers(BOB_TOKEN)\n    assert_response :forbidden\n  end\nend\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/images/sandbox-test/entrypoint.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\nset -e\n\nUSERNAME=\"${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}\"\nSSH_KEY=\"${SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY:-}\"\n\n# Create user if not exists\nif ! id \"$USERNAME\" &>/dev/null; then\n    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo \"$USERNAME\"\n    echo \"$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL\" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle\n    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle\nfi\n\n# Append SSH key (don't overwrite — multiple sandboxes may share home dir)\nif [ -n \"$SSH_KEY\" ]; then\n    SSH_DIR=\"/home/$USERNAME/.ssh\"\n    mkdir -p \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    if [ -f \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" ]; then\n        grep -qF \"$SSH_KEY\" \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" || echo \"$SSH_KEY\" >> \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    else\n        echo \"$SSH_KEY\" > \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n    fi\n    chmod 700 \"$SSH_DIR\"\n    chmod 600 \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\nfi\n\n# Ownership and permissions required by sshd StrictModes\nchown -R \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" \"/home/$USERNAME\" 2>/dev/null || true\nchmod 755 \"/home/$USERNAME\"\n\n# Generate SSH host keys if missing\nssh-keygen -A\n\n# Start SSH daemon in foreground\nexec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e\n"
}

    99→    # Directories bind-mounted into Sysbox containers must be world-writable
